# Unique Delta Force Portal (`udf21`) — Fix Verification & Regression Audit Report

**Target Repository:** Unique Delta Force HR & Workforce Management Portal (`udf21`, commit `8cd6ec2e4bd0ec6cb3bb0c3b4e71b1147cc96607`)  
**Hostnames Covered:** `udfonline.in`, `admin.udfonline.in`, `employee.udfonline.in`  
**Baseline Reports Audited:** `complete_audit_report.md` (`UDF-AUD-01`–`UDF-AUD-320`) & `audit_report_part2_321_to_420.md` (`UDF-AUD-321`–`UDF-AUD-420`)  
**Audit Scope:** Line-by-line fix verification of all **420 prior findings**, deep regression and new-vulnerability audit (**40 new findings: `UDF-AUD-421`–`UDF-AUD-460`**), and static syntax/rules/import integrity verification.

## 1. Overall Fix Verification & Regression Summary

### Prior Findings Verification (`UDF-AUD-01` – `UDF-AUD-420`)

| Severity | Total | FIXED | PARTIALLY FIXED | UNFIXED | Fix Rate (Full) |
| :--- | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: |
| **CRITICAL** | 22 | 13 | 7 | 2 | 59.1% |
| **HIGH** | 169 | 54 | 81 | 34 | 32.0% |
| **MEDIUM** | 196 | 95 | 50 | 51 | 48.5% |
| **LOW** | 33 | 17 | 12 | 4 | 51.5% |
| **TOTAL** | **420** | **179** (42.6%) | **150** (35.7%) | **91** (21.7%) | **42.6%** |

### Newly Discovered Regressions & Findings (`UDF-AUD-421` – `UDF-AUD-460`)

| Severity | Count | Key Themes |
| :--- | ---: | :--- |
| **CRITICAL** | **6** | Drive Apps Script auth bypass on new actions, `bgv_records` vendor escalation, Level-1 workflow skip via `targetLevel`, `selfLinkGoogleAccount` horizontal hijack, Google unlink auto-relink via `googleUid`, `DataCorrectionPage` file-upload validation regression |
| **HIGH** | **21** | `changeEmployeePassword` undefined `recordFailedAttempt` crash, `token.provider_id` anonymous bypass, `syncStaffGoogleLogin` claims-wipe race, `bulkImportEmployeesServer` 800-op batch crash, `bgvService` `initiatedDate` silent empty list, `AuthGuard` infinite redirect loop, `uploadProfilePhoto` path rejection, fail-open site scoping & missing admin route guards |
| **MEDIUM** | **13** | `completeTrainingQuizServer` points race/loss, `promoteEmployeeServer` `assignedSiteIds` overwrite, `syncSubmissionToSheetsServer` false-synced lock, `AdminWebsiteManager` attendance rules mismatch, `vite.config.ts` outDir/PWA mismatch, `AuthContext` listener leak, `markAllRead` portalId skip |
| **LOW** | **0** | None |
| **TOTAL NEW** | **40** | **`UDF-AUD-421` through `UDF-AUD-460`** |

## 2. Syntax, Rules & Build Integrity Check

| Check | Target(s) | Result | Details |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | 
| Node.js Syntax Check (`node --check`) | `functions/index.js` (9,191 lines) | **PASS** | Zero JS syntax errors; 59 exported functions, 0 duplicate exports. *(Note: runtime `ReferenceError` exists at `L1175` for undefined `recordFailedAttempt` — see `UDF-AUD-429`.)* |
| Node.js Syntax Check (`node --check`) | `functions/googleFormMapping.js`, `public/sw.js`, `scripts/*.js` (6 scripts) | **PASS** | All 8 scripts pass `node --check` cleanly. |
| Firestore Rules Structural Check | `firestore.rules` (677 lines) | **PASS (Syntax) / WARN (CEL Runtime)** | Balanced braces (`86/86`) and parens (`902/902`). **Runtime CEL bugs found:** `leave_balances` `allow write` references `request.resource.data.siteId` on `delete` (`UDF-AUD-428`), and `correction_requests` checks `EMPLOYEES_CREATE_CORRECTION_LINK` which is missing from `FIRESTORE_RULES_PERMS` (`UDF-AUD-430`). |
| Storage Rules Structural Check | `storage.rules` (47 lines) | **PASS** | Balanced braces (`8/8`) and parens (`3/3`). Client writes remain denied; uploads flow via `getUploadUrl`. |
| Firebase Hosting & Config JSON | `firebase.json` | **PASS** | Valid JSON with keys `firestore`, `storage`, `functions`, `hosting`, `auth`. |
| TypeScript / TSX Import Integrity | 138 `.ts` / `.tsx` files across `src/`, `components/`, `hooks/`, `utils/`, root | **PASS** | 0 broken relative imports across all 138 TypeScript/TSX files. *(Note: `node_modules` is absent in the repository snapshot, so full `tsc --noEmit` / `vite build` requires `npm install`.)* |
| Static Service Worker & Vite Config | `public/sw.js`, `vite.config.ts` | **WARN** | `public/sw.js` (14 lines) still exists and forces `clients.forEach(c => c.navigate(c.url))` (`UDF-AUD-170` UNFIXED); `vite.config.ts` `generate-version-json` writes to `dist-public` while default `outDir` is `dist` (`UDF-AUD-448`). |

## 3. Action Queue — Remaining Unfixed & Partially Fixed Prior Findings

All **241 remaining prior findings** (`91 UNFIXED` + `150 PARTIALLY FIXED`) are grouped below by severity for immediate remediation tracking.

### 3.1 Remaining CRITICAL Findings (9 Total: 2 UNFIXED, 7 PARTIALLY FIXED)

| ID | Status | File(s) & Lines (`udf21`) | Remaining Gap / Evidence |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `UDF-AUD-34` | **UNFIXED** | `components/employee/EmployeeAccountUpdatePage.tsx`, `components/EmployeeDashboardPage.tsx` (EmployeeAccountUpdatePage.tsx:31-44, EmployeeDashboardPage.tsx:213-221) | EmployeeAccountUpdatePage.tsx:33 still computes `editableFields` exclusively from `(employeeData?.originalFormSections \|\| [])`, and EmployeeDashboardPage.tsx:213-221 still does not pass `formSections` as a fallback prop, so employees without `originalFormSections` on their `employees` doc still see zero editable fields. |
| `UDF-AUD-136` | **UNFIXED** | `components/AdminWebsiteManager.tsx`, `firestore.rules` (AdminWebsiteManager.tsx:1184-1201; firestore.rules) | AdminWebsiteManager.tsx:1184-1201 still displays Emergency Lock (`manualEmergencyLock`), Read-Only Mode (`manualReadOnly`), and circuit-breaker cap controls, while `firestore.rules` contains zero references to `manualEmergencyLock`, `manualReadOnly`, or usage cap fields. |
| `UDF-AUD-03` | **PARTIALLY FIXED** | `functions/index.js` (functions/index.js:1214-1359, 1424-1504, 1622-1771) | FIXED parts: (1) selfLinkGoogleAccount (L1631-1642) now requires request.data.googleIdToken and rejects missing tokens; (2) both linkEmployeeGoogleAccount (L1333-1338) and selfLinkGoogleAccount (L1744-1750) now write linkedUid/googleUid without overwriting primary uid; (3) selfLinkGoogleAccount (L1744-1750) no longer wipes passwordHash to ''. UNFIXED parts: (1) linkEmployeeGoogleAccount (L1223-1240) still allows omitting idToken when request.auth.uid exists (falling back to client-supplied email if request.auth.token.email is absent) and never checks decodedToken.email_verified === false (nor does selfLinkGoogleAccount at L1633); (2) linkEmployeeGoogleAccount still never verifies request.auth owns employeeId; (3) unlinkEmployeeGoogleAccount (L1424-1504) was restricted to admin claims (L1430-1439) instead of employee owner, still lacks isDisabled/permission/site checks, and omits deleting googleUid. |
| `UDF-AUD-05` | **PARTIALLY FIXED** | `firestore.rules`, `components/employee/DataCorrectionPage.tsx`, `functions/index.js` (firestore.rules:146-151, 204-211, 251-254, 556-560; components/employee/DataCorrectionPage.tsx:168-174, 196; functions/index.js:2640-2645, 3861-3866, 3902-3907) | FIXED in firestore.rules & DataCorrectionPage.tsx: `hasActiveCorrectionToken(resource.data)` was removed from `allow get` on `employees` (L204-211) and `onboarding_submissions` (L251-254) (helper remains unused at L146-151), `correction_requests` `allow get` (L558) now requires `isAdmin() \|\| hasPermission(...)`, and `DataCorrectionPage.tsx` calls `getCorrectionChallengeClient(token)` first (L168) and gates target `getDoc` behind `isPostVerify \|\| guestVerified \|\| !isAnonymous` (L196). UNFIXED in functions/index.js: `deleteStoragePath` (L2640-2645) and `getUploadUrl` (L3861-3866, L3902-3907) still authorize Storage uploads/deletions solely when `activeCorrectionToken` points to a `status === 'pending'` `correction_requests` doc without requiring a verified `sessionToken`. |
| `UDF-AUD-06` | **PARTIALLY FIXED** | `firestore.rules`, `src/services/bgvService.ts`, `components/AdminWebsiteManager.tsx`, `components/AdminInvitesPage.tsx` (firestore.rules:212-246, 255-273, 321, 340, 351, 370, 381, 400, 405, 412-413, 507, 522-524, 529, 540, 617-620; src/services/bgvService.ts:27-31; components/AdminWebsiteManager.tsx:140; components/AdminInvitesPage.tsx:328-334) | FIXED in most collections: `canStaffViewSite(...)` was added to `allow list/update/create/delete` on `employees`, `onboarding_submissions`, `leave_requests`, `account_updates`, `resignations`, `attendance`, and `bgv_records` read/staff-update; `bgvService.ts:27-31` now accepts `allowedSiteIds` and `AdminInvitesPage.tsx:328-334` scopes queries by `sites`. REMAINING GAPS: (1) `firestore.rules:413` (`disciplinary_actions` `allow delete`) completely omits `canStaffViewSite`, and `L412` (`allow update`) checks `request.resource.data.get('siteId', '')` instead of `resource.data.get('siteId', '')`; (2) `firestore.rules:522-524` (`bgv_records` `allow create`) omits `canStaffViewSite` and `L540` leaves `\|\| hasPermission('BGV_VENDOR')` unscoped; (3) `AdminWebsiteManager.tsx:140` still queries `collection(db, 'attendance')` without site scoping; (4) all rules include `siteId == '' \|\| ...`, permitting cross-site access to records with empty/missing `siteId`. |
| `UDF-AUD-25` | **PARTIALLY FIXED** | `functions/index.js`, `src/utils/bulkImportUtils.ts`, `src/services/employeeService.ts` (functions/index.js:8333, 8399-8474; src/utils/bulkImportUtils.ts:798-803; src/services/employeeService.ts:1321-1326, 1435-1441) | FIXED parts: (1) bulkImportEmployeesServer now writes employees/{submissionId} (L8455) matching onboarding_submissions/{submissionId} (L8456); (2) employeeRecord now populates assignedSiteIds, activeAssignments, needsProfileCompletion: null, and data (L8411-8439), and getPaginatedEmployees (employeeService.ts:1321-1323, 1437) moved ACTIVE needsProfileCompletion filtering in-memory; (3) bulkImportUtils.ts:800 now passes importMode and defaultStatus in options. UNFIXED parts: (1) bulkImportEmployeesServer (L8333, L8413) ignores options.importMode and hardcodes needsProfileCompletion: null (breaking PENDING/INCOMPLETE imports); (2) default password at L8399-8401 still uses `${row.fullName.split(' ')[0]}@${rawPhone \|\| rawDob.slice(0, 4)}` instead of lowercase firstnameYYYY@last4; (3) L8431/L8438 still only spreads row.formData (undefined for flat CSV rows). |
| `UDF-AUD-46` | **PARTIALLY FIXED** | `components/AdminAuditLogPage.tsx`, `src/services/auditService.ts`, `firestore.rules` (AdminAuditLogPage.tsx:14,21-83,94-97,110-124; auditService.ts:91-101,118-127; firestore.rules:449-452) | AdminAuditLogPage.tsx:14 now destructures `{ user } = useAuth()`, L94 resolves currentUid safely, and L110-120 guards all `.toLowerCase()` calls with String(... \|\| "") fallbacks across description/actorEmail/category/action. However, the hidden privilege mode (L21-83) calling _deleteAuditLog/_updateAuditLogDetails remains in the client even though firestore.rules:451 strictly denies client update/delete on audit_logs. |
| `UDF-AUD-135` | **PARTIALLY FIXED** | `components/AdminBgvManagementPage.tsx` (AdminBgvManagementPage.tsx:245-270,814-822) | AdminBgvManagementPage.tsx:245-249 now fails closed when `allowedSiteIds.length === 0` or `!record.siteId`, and L253-255 blocks undispatched records (`!record.vendorName && !record.vendorId`) from external vendors. However, table avatars at L814-822 still render `item.photoUrl` unconditionally without checking `isFieldHiddenForRole("photo")`. |
| `UDF-AUD-325` | **PARTIALLY FIXED** | `functions/index.js` (functions/index.js:1574-1611) | PARTIALLY FIXED: `setupEmployeePassword` added an ownership check at L1574-1580 (`if (verifiedEmployeeId && verifiedEmployeeId !== inputId)`). However, if the caller's own `onboarding_submissions/{uid}` or `employees/{uid}` doc has no `employeeId` yet (`verifiedEmployeeId === ''`), the `if` condition is skipped, L1594 falls back to `cleanId = inputId` (attacker-supplied victim ID), and L1595-1610 updates all victim `employees`/`onboarding_submissions` matching `data.employeeId === cleanId`. |

## 5. Newly Discovered Regressions & Findings (`UDF-AUD-421` – `UDF-AUD-460`)

### 5.1 Executive Summary Table (`UDF-AUD-421` – `UDF-AUD-460`)

| ID | Severity | Category | Target File(s) | Exact Lines | Summary |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `UDF-AUD-421` | **CRITICAL** | Security Vulnerability / Authorization Regression | `GOOGLE_APPS_SCRIPT_v7.2.js` | `GOOGLE_APPS_SCRIPT_v7.2.js:111-128, 145-146` | Newly added `relieve_employee` and `rejoin_employee` Drive actions in `GOOGLE_APPS_SCRIPT_v7.2.js` are omitted from `adminActions`, allowing any anonymous Firebase user to move arbitrary employee Drive folders |
| `UDF-AUD-422` | **CRITICAL** | Security Vulnerability / Privilege Escalation Regression | `firestore.rules` | `firestore.rules:508-514, 533-545` | `bgv_records` `allow update` retains standalone `\|\| hasPermission('BGV_VENDOR')` bypass and leaves `vendorId`/`vendorName` mutable, enabling vendor record hijacking and full PII read escalation |
| `UDF-AUD-423` | **CRITICAL** | Security Vulnerability / Regression | `functions/index.js` | `functions/index.js:5086, 5178-5182, 5360-5382` | processRequestReviewServer allows any Level 1 approver to bypass all higher workflow levels via untrusted request.data.targetLevel >= maxLevels |
| `UDF-AUD-424` | **CRITICAL** | Security Vulnerability / Regression | `functions/index.js` | `functions/index.js:588-591, 611-614, 692-719, 1490-1496, 1833-1848` | Google account unlinking (unlinkEmployeeGoogleAccount & adminControlEmployeeAuth) leaves `googleUid` intact while employeeGoogleLogin queries `googleUid` and auto-relinks the unlinked account |
| `UDF-AUD-425` | **CRITICAL** | Security Vulnerability / Horizontal Privilege Escalation | `functions/index.js` | `functions/index.js:1656-1699, 1752-1762` | `selfLinkGoogleAccount` overwrites `linkedUid`/`linkedGoogleEmail` on all `employees` and `onboarding_submissions` matching caller-supplied `employeeId` without verifying ownership |
| `UDF-AUD-426` | **CRITICAL** | Broken Feature / Regression | `components/employee/DataCorrectionPage.tsx` | `components/employee/DataCorrectionPage.tsx:402-404, 644-656, 735-739` | DataCorrectionPage File Upload Validation Rejects All Local `File` Objects as Empty (Regression From UDF-AUD-74 Patch) |
| `UDF-AUD-427` | **HIGH** | Security Vulnerability / Authorization Regression | `firestore.rules` | `firestore.rules:434-440` | `notifications` `allow update` permits any authenticated or anonymous user to arbitrarily overwrite or clear `readBy` on any notification document |
| `UDF-AUD-428` | **HIGH** | Security Vulnerability & CEL Runtime Bug / Regression | `firestore.rules` | `firestore.rules:412, 468-472` | `leave_balances` `allow write` and `disciplinary_actions` `allow update` check `request.resource.data.siteId` instead of existing `resource.data.siteId`, enabling cross-site overwrite and crashing `leave_balances` deletes |
| `UDF-AUD-429` | **HIGH** | Rules / Claims Mismatch Regression | `firestore.rules`, `functions/index.js` | `firestore.rules:558-559; functions/index.js:4170-4191` | `firestore.rules` checks `hasPermission('EMPLOYEES_CREATE_CORRECTION_LINK')` on `correction_requests`, but `FIRESTORE_RULES_PERMS` in `functions/index.js` omits it |
| `UDF-AUD-430` | **HIGH** | Regression / Runtime Crash & Missing Rate Limit | `functions/index.js` | `functions/index.js:1166-1179` | changeEmployeePassword calls undefined `recordFailedAttempt`, crashing with ReferenceError on wrong passwords and providing zero brute-force rate limiting |
| `UDF-AUD-431` | **HIGH** | Regression / Trigger Race Condition | `functions/index.js` | `functions/index.js:3694-3705, 3716-3726, 4371-4406` | syncStaffGoogleLogin deleting `originalDoc` triggers handleStaffOrUserWritten to wipe all Firebase Auth custom claims of the newly synced staff user |
| `UDF-AUD-432` | **HIGH** | Regression / Identity Integrity | `functions/index.js` | `functions/index.js:1273-1289, 1333-1338, 1721-1741` | linkEmployeeGoogleAccount and selfLinkGoogleAccount uniqueness checks omit `linkedUid` and `googleUid` (and linkEmployeeGoogleAccount stores un-normalized email), allowing duplicate Google account bindings |
| `UDF-AUD-433` | **HIGH** | Security Vulnerability / Authorization Bypass | `functions/index.js` | `functions/index.js:3962-4044` | getUploadUrl `context === 'admin'` fallback branch lets any active staff user (including read-only viewers and BGV vendors) upload to arbitrary non-`public/`/`employees/` Storage paths |
| `UDF-AUD-434` | **HIGH** | Regression / Security Bypass | `functions/index.js` | `functions/index.js:79-81, 100-102` | `hashCandidatePasswordServer` and `checkGlobalUniqueness` check non-existent `token.provider_id === 'anonymous'` instead of `token.firebase.sign_in_provider === 'anonymous'` |
| `UDF-AUD-435` | **HIGH** | Security Vulnerability / Authorization Bypass | `functions/index.js` | `functions/index.js:9041-9044, 9057, 9071-9075, 9115-9146` | `completeBgvReviewServer` trusts caller-supplied `employeeId` over `bgvData.employeeId` and allows any `BGV_UPLOAD_CERTIFICATE` holder (including vendors) to approve/reject BGV |

### 5.2 Detailed Breakdown & Copy-Paste `diff` Patches (`UDF-AUD-421` – `UDF-AUD-460`)

#### `UDF-AUD-421` — **CRITICAL** | Security Vulnerability / Authorization Regression — Newly added `relieve_employee` and `rejoin_employee` Drive actions in `GOOGLE_APPS_SCRIPT_v7.2.js` are omitted from `adminActions`, allowing any anonymous Firebase user to move arbitrary employee Drive folders

- **Target File(s):** `GOOGLE_APPS_SCRIPT_v7.2.js` (`GOOGLE_APPS_SCRIPT_v7.2.js:111-128, 145-146`)
- **Root Cause & Exact Line Trace:** When adding `relieve_employee` (L145) and `rejoin_employee` (L146) to `doPost` to address UDF-AUD-198, both action names were omitted from the `adminActions` array at L111-123. As a result, `isAdministrative` (L125) evaluates to `false`, completely bypassing the `authInfo.isSuperAdmin` check at L126.
- **Real-World Impact:** Any authenticated Firebase caller — including an unauthenticated public visitor signed in anonymously via `ensureAuthenticated()` (`signInAnonymously`) — can send a POST request with `action: 'relieve_employee'` or `'rejoin_employee'` and relocate any employee's Google Drive folder into `Relieved Employees` or `Active Employees`.
- **Copy-Paste `diff` Remediation:**

```diff
--- a/GOOGLE_APPS_SCRIPT_v7.2.js
+++ b/GOOGLE_APPS_SCRIPT_v7.2.js
@@ -119,7 +119,9 @@
       'restore_employee',
       'permanent_delete_employee',
       'block_employee',
-      'unblock_employee'
+      'unblock_employee',
+      'relieve_employee',
+      'rejoin_employee'
     ];
```

#### `UDF-AUD-422` — **CRITICAL** | Security Vulnerability / Privilege Escalation Regression — `bgv_records` `allow update` retains standalone `|| hasPermission('BGV_VENDOR')` bypass and leaves `vendorId`/`vendorName` mutable, enabling vendor record hijacking and full PII read escalation

- **Target File(s):** `firestore.rules` (`firestore.rules:508-514, 533-545`)
- **Root Cause & Exact Line Trace:** While `bgv_records` `allow read` (L508-514) was tightened to require `vendorId == request.auth.uid` or matching `vendorName`, `allow update` (L534-541) still includes a standalone `|| hasPermission('BGV_VENDOR')` at L540 outside the `vendorId`/`vendorName` ownership check, and L542 does not include `vendorId` or `vendorName` in the immutable `affectedKeys` denylist.
- **Real-World Impact:** Any external BGV vendor holding `BGV_VENDOR` can call `updateDoc` on any undispatched or competitor-assigned `bgv_records` document to set `vendorId: request.auth.uid`, which then immediately satisfies `allow read` (L509) and leaks the employee's full Aadhaar, phone number, and BGV dossier.
- **Copy-Paste `diff` Remediation:**

```diff
--- a/firestore.rules
+++ b/firestore.rules
@@ -534,12 +534,12 @@
         isAuthenticated() && (
           (resource.data.get('vendorId', '') != '' && resource.data.get('vendorId', '') == request.auth.uid) ||
           (resource.data.get('vendorName', '') != '' && (
             resource.data.get('vendorName', '') == request.auth.token.get('email', '') ||
             resource.data.get('vendorName', '') == request.auth.token.get('name', '')
-          )) ||
-          hasPermission('BGV_VENDOR')
+          ))
         ) &&
-        !request.resource.data.diff(resource.data).affectedKeys().hasAny(['approvedBy', 'approvedDate', 'rejectionReason', 'result', 'internalStatus']) &&
+        !request.resource.data.diff(resource.data).affectedKeys().hasAny(['approvedBy', 'approvedDate', 'rejectionReason', 'result', 'internalStatus', 'vendorId', 'vendorName', 'employeeDocId', 'siteId']) &&
         (!request.resource.data.diff(resource.data).affectedKeys().hasAny(['status']) ||
          request.resource.data.status in ['VENDOR_SUBMITTED', 'UNDER_PROCESS', 'PENDING', 'vendor_submitted', 'under_process', 'in-progress', 'Vendor Submitted', 'Under Process'])
       );
```

#### `UDF-AUD-423` — **CRITICAL** | Security Vulnerability / Regression — processRequestReviewServer allows any Level 1 approver to bypass all higher workflow levels via untrusted request.data.targetLevel >= maxLevels

- **Target File(s):** `functions/index.js` (`functions/index.js:5086, 5178-5182, 5360-5382`)
- **Root Cause & Exact Line Trace:** While patching UDF-AUD-29 to restrict `isBypass: true` to SuperAdmins (L5179-5182), `processRequestReviewServer` introduced `const isTargetFinal = Boolean(targetLevel && Number(targetLevel) >= maxLevels);` (L5360) and checks `if (!effectiveBypass && !isTargetFinal && currentLevel < maxLevels)` (L5361). Because `targetLevel` is destructured directly from untrusted `request.data` (L5086) and is never restricted to SuperAdmins or `REVERT` actions, any Level 1 reviewer can pass `{ action: 'APPROVE', isBypass: false, targetLevel: 99 }` to set `isTargetFinal = true` and jump straight to Final Approval.
- **Real-World Impact:** Completely defeats the UDF-AUD-29 multi-level approval fix: any Level 1 approver (e.g. Field Officer) can unilaterally final-approve multi-tier leave, resignation, or bank account update requests without HR/Operations approval.
- **Copy-Paste `diff` Remediation:**

```diff
--- a/functions/index.js
+++ b/functions/index.js
@@ -5357,8 +5357,7 @@
         }
     } else if (normalizedAction === 'APPROVE') {
-        const isTargetFinal = Boolean(targetLevel && Number(targetLevel) >= maxLevels);
-        if (!effectiveBypass && !isTargetFinal && currentLevel < maxLevels) {
+        if (!effectiveBypass && currentLevel < maxLevels) {
             // Advance to next workflow level that has an assigned role
             let nextLvl = currentLevel + 1;
```

#### `UDF-AUD-424` — **CRITICAL** | Security Vulnerability / Regression — Google account unlinking (unlinkEmployeeGoogleAccount & adminControlEmployeeAuth) leaves `googleUid` intact while employeeGoogleLogin queries `googleUid` and auto-relinks the unlinked account

- **Target File(s):** `functions/index.js` (`functions/index.js:588-591, 611-614, 692-719, 1490-1496, 1833-1848`)
- **Root Cause & Exact Line Trace:** In udf21, `linkEmployeeGoogleAccount` (L1335), `selfLinkGoogleAccount` (L1746), `approveOnboardingServer` (L5830), and `employeeGoogleLogin` (L696, L710) now write a separate `googleUid` field on both `employees` and `onboarding_submissions`, and `employeeGoogleLogin` queries `.where('googleUid', '==', googleUid)` (L588-591, L611-614). However, neither `unlinkEmployeeGoogleAccount` (L1490-1494) nor `adminControlEmployeeAuth` (`action === 'unlink_google'`, L1837-1842) deletes `googleUid` (and `unlinkEmployeeGoogleAccount` also omits revoking refresh tokens / clearing custom claims). When the unlinked Google user signs in again, `employeeGoogleLogin` matches on the lingering `googleUid` and lines 695-718 automatically rewrite `linkedUid` and `linkedGoogleEmail`, completely undoing the unlink.
- **Real-World Impact:** Unlinking a compromised or wrong Google account from an employee profile (even by a SuperAdmin in the Security Tab) is completely ineffective: the unlinked Google account can immediately sign back in and automatically re-link itself to the employee record.
- **Copy-Paste `diff` Remediation:**

```diff
--- a/functions/index.js
+++ b/functions/index.js
@@ -1490,6 +1490,7 @@
         const updates = {
             linkedUid: 'none',
+            googleUid: admin.firestore.FieldValue.delete(),
             authProvider: 'manual',
             linkedGoogleEmail: admin.firestore.FieldValue.delete()
         };
@@ -1837,6 +1838,7 @@
             const updates = {
                 uid: admin.firestore.FieldValue.delete(),
                 linkedUid: 'none',
+                googleUid: admin.firestore.FieldValue.delete(),
                 authProvider: 'manual',
                 linkedGoogleEmail: admin.firestore.FieldValue.delete()
             };
```

#### `UDF-AUD-425` — **CRITICAL** | Security Vulnerability / Horizontal Privilege Escalation — `selfLinkGoogleAccount` overwrites `linkedUid`/`linkedGoogleEmail` on all `employees` and `onboarding_submissions` matching caller-supplied `employeeId` without verifying ownership

- **Target File(s):** `functions/index.js` (`functions/index.js:1656-1699, 1752-1762`)
- **Root Cause & Exact Line Trace:** `selfLinkGoogleAccount` resolves `docRef` by `callerUid` at L1660-1672 without ever comparing `docRef`'s `employeeId` against the caller-supplied `request.data.employeeId`. It then queries `onboarding_submissions` and `employees` with `.where('employeeId', '==', cleanId)` using the untrusted `employeeId` (L1755-1762) and writes `linkPayload` (`linkedUid`, `googleUid`, `authProvider: 'google'`, `linkedGoogleEmail`) onto every matched document in the batch.
- **Real-World Impact:** Any authenticated employee can call `selfLinkGoogleAccount` with their own valid `googleIdToken` and ANY other employee's `employeeId` (e.g., `HY140001`), binding the attacker's Google account to the victim's `employees` and `onboarding_submissions` records and achieving full horizontal account takeover via `employeeGoogleLogin`.
- **Copy-Paste `diff` Remediation:**

```diff
--- a/functions/index.js
+++ b/functions/index.js
@@ -1698,6 +1698,13 @@
         if (!docRef) {
             throw new functions.https.HttpsError('permission-denied', 'You can only link Google account for your own account');
         }
+
+        const ownSnap = await docRef.get();
+        const ownEmpId = String(ownSnap.data()?.employeeId || '').trim().toUpperCase();
+        const cleanInputId = employeeId.trim().toUpperCase();
+        if (!ownEmpId || ownEmpId !== cleanInputId) {
+            throw new functions.https.HttpsError('permission-denied', 'Employee ID does not match your authenticated account.');
+        }
@@ -1752,10 +1759,10 @@
         const batch = db.batch();
         batch.update(docRef, linkPayload);
 
-        const cleanId = employeeId.trim().toUpperCase();
+        const cleanId = ownEmpId;
         const [allSubs, allEmps] = await Promise.all([
             db.collection('onboarding_submissions').where('employeeId', '==', cleanId).get(),
             db.collection('employees').where('employeeId', '==', cleanId).get()
         ]);
```

#### `UDF-AUD-426` — **CRITICAL** | Broken Feature / Regression — DataCorrectionPage File Upload Validation Rejects All Local `File` Objects as Empty (Regression From UDF-AUD-74 Patch)

- **Target File(s):** `components/employee/DataCorrectionPage.tsx` (`components/employee/DataCorrectionPage.tsx:402-404, 644-656, 735-739`)
- **Root Cause & Exact Line Trace:** In `validateSingleField` (L402-404), the `UDF-AUD-74` fix checks `const pathVal = typeof value === 'object' && value !== null ? (value.storagePath || value.url || value.fileUrl || '') : value; isEmpty = !pathVal || ...`. Because a browser `File` instance satisfies `typeof value === 'object' && value !== null` and has no `.storagePath`, `.url`, or `.fileUrl`, `pathVal` evaluates to `''` and `isEmpty` evaluates to `true`—even though L646 explicitly handles `if (value instanceof File)`. Additionally, `handleSubmit` (L738) compares `JSON.stringify(orig) !== JSON.stringify(curr)`, where `JSON.stringify(new File(...))` always produces `'{}'`.
- **Real-World Impact:** Every mandatory `FILE_UPLOAD` field on `/correct-details` fails validation with `"<Field> is required."` when a candidate or employee selects a local `File`, completely blocking document correction submissions.
- **Copy-Paste `diff` Remediation:**

```diff
--- a/components/employee/DataCorrectionPage.tsx
+++ b/components/employee/DataCorrectionPage.tsx
@@ -400,8 +400,12 @@
     if (field.type === FieldType.CHECKBOX) {
       isEmpty = !value || (Array.isArray(value) && value.length === 0);
     } else if (field.type === FieldType.FILE_UPLOAD) {
-      const pathVal = typeof value === 'object' && value !== null ? (value.storagePath || value.url || value.fileUrl || '') : value;
-      isEmpty = !pathVal || (typeof pathVal === 'string' && pathVal.trim() === '');
+      if (value instanceof File) {
+        isEmpty = value.size === 0;
+      } else {
+        const pathVal = typeof value === 'object' && value !== null ? (value.storagePath || value.url || value.fileUrl || '') : value;
+        isEmpty = !pathVal || (typeof pathVal === 'string' && pathVal.trim() === '');
+      }
     } else {
       isEmpty = value === undefined || value === null || String(value).trim() === '';
     }
@@ -735,6 +739,7 @@
     const hasAnyChange = requestDoc.requestedFields.some(fieldId => {
       const orig = originalFields[fieldId] ?? '';
       const curr = formData[fieldId] ?? '';
+      if (curr instanceof File) return true;
       return JSON.stringify(orig) !== JSON.stringify(curr);
     });
```

#### `UDF-AUD-427` — **HIGH** | Security Vulnerability / Authorization Regression — `notifications` `allow update` permits any authenticated or anonymous user to arbitrarily overwrite or clear `readBy` on any notification document

- **Target File(s):** `firestore.rules` (`firestore.rules:434-440`)
- **Root Cause & Exact Line Trace:** To support marking broadcast notifications as read (`UDF-AUD-175`), L439 added `(request.resource.data.diff(resource.data).affectedKeys().hasOnly(['readBy']))` directly under `isAuthenticated()` without checking `isRegisteredUser()`, without verifying the caller is an intended recipient (`ROLE:*` broadcast), and without restricting `readBy` mutations to appending only `request.auth.uid`.
- **Real-World Impact:** Any authenticated or anonymous user who knows or guesses a notification document ID (including private HR/admin notifications) can wipe or forge its `readBy` array, tampering with notification read receipts across the portal.
- **Copy-Paste `diff` Remediation:**

```diff
--- a/firestore.rules
+++ b/firestore.rules
@@ -435,9 +435,14 @@
       allow update: if isAdmin() || (isAuthenticated() && (
         resource.data.targetUserId == request.auth.uid ||
         (request.auth.token.get('employeeId', '') != '' && resource.data.targetUserId == request.auth.token.employeeId) ||
         isOwnerOfPortalId(resource.data.targetUserId) ||
-        (request.resource.data.diff(resource.data).affectedKeys().hasOnly(['readBy']))
+        (
+          isRegisteredUser() &&
+          resource.data.targetUserId in [('ROLE:' + getUserRoleId()), 'ROLE:employee', 'ROLE:role-security-guard'] &&
+          request.resource.data.diff(resource.data).affectedKeys().hasOnly(['readBy']) &&
+          request.resource.data.readBy.hasAll(resource.data.get('readBy', [])) &&
+          request.resource.data.readBy.size() <= resource.data.get('readBy', []).size() + 1
+        )
       ));
```

#### `UDF-AUD-428` — **HIGH** | Security Vulnerability & CEL Runtime Bug / Regression — `leave_balances` `allow write` and `disciplinary_actions` `allow update` check `request.resource.data.siteId` instead of existing `resource.data.siteId`, enabling cross-site overwrite and crashing `leave_balances` deletes

- **Target File(s):** `firestore.rules` (`firestore.rules:412, 468-472`)
- **Root Cause & Exact Line Trace:** In `disciplinary_actions` (L412), `allow create, update` validates `canStaffViewSite(request.resource.data.get('siteId', ''))` (the incoming payload) rather than existing `resource.data.get('siteId', '')` on updates. In `leave_balances` (L468-472), `allow write` (covering create, update, and delete) checks `request.resource.data.get('siteId', '')` — ignoring existing `resource.data.siteId` on updates and dereferencing `request.resource.data` when `request.resource == null` on deletes, which throws a CEL runtime evaluation error.
- **Real-World Impact:** A site-scoped supervisor from Site A can modify any Site B employee's `disciplinary_actions` or `leave_balances` document by setting `siteId: ''` or `siteId: 'site-A'` in the update payload; additionally, `leave_balances` deletes by non-SuperAdmin staff with `LEAVES_MANAGE_BALANCES` always fail with `PERMISSION_DENIED`.
- **Copy-Paste `diff` Remediation:**

```diff
--- a/firestore.rules
+++ b/firestore.rules
@@ -409,7 +409,10 @@
                        isOwnerOfPortalId(resource.data.employeeId) ||
                        (resource.data.get('portalId', '') != '' && isOwnerOfPortalId(resource.data.portalId))
                      ));
-      allow create, update: if (hasPermission('DISCIPLINE_MANAGE') || hasPermission('DISCIPLINE_CREATE') || hasPermission('DISCIPLINE_ISSUE') || isSuperAdmin()) && (request.resource.data.get('siteId', '') == '' || canStaffViewSite(request.resource.data.get('siteId', '')));
+      allow create: if (hasPermission('DISCIPLINE_MANAGE') || hasPermission('DISCIPLINE_CREATE') || hasPermission('DISCIPLINE_ISSUE') || isSuperAdmin()) && (request.resource.data.get('siteId', '') == '' || canStaffViewSite(request.resource.data.get('siteId', '')));
+      allow update: if (hasPermission('DISCIPLINE_MANAGE') || hasPermission('DISCIPLINE_CREATE') || hasPermission('DISCIPLINE_ISSUE') || isSuperAdmin()) &&
+        (resource.data.get('siteId', '') == '' || canStaffViewSite(resource.data.get('siteId', ''))) &&
+        (request.resource.data.get('siteId', '') == '' || canStaffViewSite(request.resource.data.get('siteId', '')));
       allow delete: if hasPermission('DISCIPLINE_MANAGE') || hasPermission('DISCIPLINE_DELETE') || isSuperAdmin();
     }
@@ -465,11 +468,15 @@
         isOwnerOfPortalId(id) || 
         isOwnerOfPortalId(resource.data.employeeId)
       ));
-      allow write: if isSuperAdmin() || (
-        isAdmin() && 
-        hasPermission('LEAVES_MANAGE_BALANCES') && 
-        (request.resource.data.get('siteId', '') == '' || canStaffViewSite(request.resource.data.get('siteId', '')))
-      );
+      allow create: if isSuperAdmin() || (
+        isAdmin() && hasPermission('LEAVES_MANAGE_BALANCES') &&
+        (request.resource.data.get('siteId', '') == '' || canStaffViewSite(request.resource.data.get('siteId', '')))
+      );
+      allow update: if isSuperAdmin() || (
+        isAdmin() && hasPermission('LEAVES_MANAGE_BALANCES') &&
+        (resource.data.get('siteId', '') == '' || canStaffViewSite(resource.data.get('siteId', ''))) &&
+        (request.resource.data.get('siteId', '') == '' || canStaffViewSite(request.resource.data.get('siteId', '')))
+      );
+      allow delete: if isSuperAdmin() || (
+        isAdmin() && hasPermission('LEAVES_MANAGE_BALANCES') &&
+        (resource.data.get('siteId', '') == '' || canStaffViewSite(resource.data.get('siteId', '')))
+      );
     }
```

- **Real-World Impact:** The `EMPLOYEES_CREATE_CORRECTION_LINK` permission is never present in `request.auth.token.permissions`, rendering that rule branch ineffective for non-admin/delegated roles.

```diff
-        'EMPLOYEES_MARK_PROFILE_COMPLETE', 'EMPLOYEES_TAB_RECYCLE_BIN', 'EMPLOYEES_CORRECT_ID',
```

#### `UDF-AUD-430` — **HIGH** | Regression / Runtime Crash & Missing Rate Limit — changeEmployeePassword calls undefined `recordFailedAttempt`, crashing with ReferenceError on wrong passwords and providing zero brute-force rate limiting

- **Target File(s):** `functions/index.js` (`functions/index.js:1166-1179`)
- **Root Cause & Exact Line Trace:** Line 1175 invokes `await recordFailedAttempt(db, `pwchange_${callerUid}`).catch(() => {});`, but `recordFailedAttempt` is never defined anywhere in `functions/index.js` (the helper is `checkAndIncrementFailedAttempts` at L484). Because invoking an undefined identifier throws a synchronous `ReferenceError` before a Promise is created, `.catch(() => {})` does not catch it; execution jumps to the outer `catch` (L1206), returning an opaque `internal` error instead of `permission-denied: 'Incorrect current password'`. Additionally, `changeEmployeePassword` never checks the lockout state prior to verifying the password, leaving it unprotected against brute-force attempts.
- **Real-World Impact:** Every employee who mistypes their current password in Security Settings receives an `internal` server error instead of `'Incorrect current password'`, and authenticated sessions can brute-force passwords without hitting any lockout.
- **Copy-Paste `diff` Remediation:**

```diff
--- a/functions/index.js
+++ b/functions/index.js
@@ -1163,6 +1163,11 @@
             throw new functions.https.HttpsError('permission-denied', 'You do not have permission to change this password.');
         }
 
+        const isLocked = await checkAndIncrementFailedAttempts(db, `pwchange_${callerUid}`);
+        if (isLocked) {
+            throw new functions.https.HttpsError('resource-exhausted', 'Too many failed attempts. Try again in 1 hour.');
+        }
+
         // SECURITY: Use bcrypt verify. Also handle legacy SHA-256 hashes.
         let isVerified = false;
         if (empData.passwordHash) {
@@ -1172,7 +1177,6 @@
         // All employees must use their hashed password or go through reset flow.
 
         if (!isVerified) {
-            await recordFailedAttempt(db, `pwchange_${callerUid}`).catch(() => {});
             throw new functions.https.HttpsError('permission-denied', 'Incorrect current password');
         }
```

#### `UDF-AUD-431` — **HIGH** | Regression / Trigger Race Condition — syncStaffGoogleLogin deleting `originalDoc` triggers handleStaffOrUserWritten to wipe all Firebase Auth custom claims of the newly synced staff user

- **Target File(s):** `functions/index.js` (`functions/index.js:3694-3705, 3716-3726, 4371-4406`)
- **Root Cause & Exact Line Trace:** As part of fixing UDF-AUD-332, `syncStaffGoogleLogin` now copies `originalDoc` to `staff_accounts/{uid}` (L3694) or `users/{uid}` (L3716) and then executes `await originalDoc.ref.delete()` (L3702, L3723). Deleting `originalDoc` fires `onStaffAccountWritten` / `onUserAccountWritten` (`handleStaffOrUserWritten`, L4371-4406). Because `afterData` is undefined, L4388 fails `getUser(originalDoc.id)` and L4389-4393 falls back to `admin.auth().getUserByEmail(beforeData.email)`, which resolves to the newly synced staff user's `uid`! Line 4396-4397 then sees `!event.data?.after?.exists` is true and executes `await admin.auth().setCustomUserClaims(authUid, {})` without checking whether a live document now exists at `staff_accounts/{authUid}` or `users/{authUid}`.
- **Real-World Impact:** Whenever a pre-provisioned staff member or SuperAdmin logs in with Google for the first time and `syncStaffGoogleLogin` migrates their document ID to their Google UID, the deletion trigger races and wipes all of their admin custom claims (`{}`), immediately locking them out of Firestore and Cloud Functions.
- **Copy-Paste `diff` Remediation:**

```diff
--- a/functions/index.js
+++ b/functions/index.js
@@ -4394,8 +4394,14 @@
 
         if (authUser) {
             if (!event.data?.after?.exists) {
-                await admin.auth().setCustomUserClaims(authUid, {});
-                console.log(`[TRIGGER: handleStaffOrUserWritten] Cleared claims for deleted doc ${collectionName}/${authUid}`);
+                // Guard against syncStaffGoogleLogin migration: do not wipe claims if an active doc exists under authUid
+                const activeDoc = await admin.firestore().collection(collectionName).doc(authUid).get();
+                if (activeDoc.exists && staffId !== authUid) {
+                    console.log(`[TRIGGER: handleStaffOrUserWritten] Skipping claim clear for migrated doc ${collectionName}/${staffId} -> ${authUid}`);
+                } else {
+                    await admin.auth().setCustomUserClaims(authUid, {});
+                    console.log(`[TRIGGER: handleStaffOrUserWritten] Cleared claims for deleted doc ${collectionName}/${authUid}`);
+                }
             } else {
                 await computeAndApplyUserClaims(authUid, staffId, afterData);
```

#### `UDF-AUD-432` — **HIGH** | Regression / Identity Integrity — linkEmployeeGoogleAccount and selfLinkGoogleAccount uniqueness checks omit `linkedUid` and `googleUid` (and linkEmployeeGoogleAccount stores un-normalized email), allowing duplicate Google account bindings

- **Target File(s):** `functions/index.js` (`functions/index.js:1273-1289, 1333-1338, 1721-1741`)
- **Root Cause & Exact Line Trace:** After removing `uid: googleUid` writes to fix UDF-AUD-03, `linkEmployeeGoogleAccount` (L1274-1280) and `selfLinkGoogleAccount` (L1721-1726) still check for existing employee collisions using `employees.where('uid', '==', googleUid)` and `onboarding_submissions.where('uid', '==', googleUid)`, omitting `employees.where('linkedUid', '==', googleUid)`, `employees.where('googleUid', '==', googleUid)`, and `onboarding_submissions.where('googleUid', '==', googleUid)` (and `selfLinkGoogleAccount` also omits `onboarding_submissions.where('linkedUid', '==', googleUid)`). Furthermore, `linkEmployeeGoogleAccount` (L1337) writes raw un-normalized `linkedGoogleEmail: email` instead of `normalizedGoogleEmail`, breaking case-sensitive `where('linkedGoogleEmail', '==', normalizedGoogleEmail)` checks.
- **Real-World Impact:** The same Google account can be linked to multiple different employee records (especially when email casing varies or the Google email changes), causing `employeeGoogleLogin` to log the user into an unpredictable employee profile.
- **Copy-Paste `diff` Remediation:**

```diff
--- a/functions/index.js
+++ b/functions/index.js
@@ -1273,9 +1273,12 @@
         // Check if it belongs to another Employee
         const empChecks = await Promise.all([
             db.collection('employees').where('uid', '==', googleUid).get(),
+            db.collection('employees').where('linkedUid', '==', googleUid).get(),
+            db.collection('employees').where('googleUid', '==', googleUid).get(),
             db.collection('employees').where('linkedGoogleEmail', '==', normalizedGoogleEmail).get(),
             db.collection('onboarding_submissions').where('uid', '==', googleUid).get(),
             db.collection('onboarding_submissions').where('linkedUid', '==', googleUid).get(),
+            db.collection('onboarding_submissions').where('googleUid', '==', googleUid).get(),
             db.collection('onboarding_submissions').where('linkedGoogleEmail', '==', normalizedGoogleEmail).get()
         ]);
@@ -1334,7 +1337,7 @@
             linkedUid: googleUid,
             googleUid: googleUid,
             authProvider: 'google',
-            linkedGoogleEmail: email
+            linkedGoogleEmail: normalizedGoogleEmail
         };
@@ -1721,9 +1724,13 @@
         const empChecks = await Promise.all([
             db.collection('employees').where('uid', '==', googleUid).get(),
+            db.collection('employees').where('linkedUid', '==', googleUid).get(),
+            db.collection('employees').where('googleUid', '==', googleUid).get(),
             db.collection('employees').where('linkedGoogleEmail', '==', normalizedGoogleEmail).get(),
             db.collection('onboarding_submissions').where('uid', '==', googleUid).get(),
+            db.collection('onboarding_submissions').where('linkedUid', '==', googleUid).get(),
+            db.collection('onboarding_submissions').where('googleUid', '==', googleUid).get(),
             db.collection('onboarding_submissions').where('linkedGoogleEmail', '==', normalizedGoogleEmail).get()
         ]);
```

#### `UDF-AUD-433` — **HIGH** | Security Vulnerability / Authorization Bypass — getUploadUrl `context === 'admin'` fallback branch lets any active staff user (including read-only viewers and BGV vendors) upload to arbitrary non-`public/`/`employees/` Storage paths

- **Target File(s):** `functions/index.js` (`functions/index.js:3962-4044`)
- **Root Cause & Exact Line Trace:** In `getUploadUrl`, the `context === 'admin'` handler restricts `public/*` (L3969-3972) and site-scopes `employees/*` (L3973-4031), but its catch-all `else` branch (L4032-4042, 'Other general admin assets') authorizes ANY active document in `staff_accounts` or `users` with zero permission, role, or site checks. Consequently, any low-privilege staff member or external BGV vendor can pass `context: 'admin'` with `storagePath: 'onboarding/{anyApplicant}/...'`, `'draft_submissions/...'`, `'users/...'`, or `'site_assets/...'` and receive a signed write URL.
- **Real-World Impact:** Low-privilege staff and external BGV vendors can overwrite candidate onboarding documents, drafts, or site assets across any branch without holding any upload or site-management permission.
- **Copy-Paste `diff` Remediation:**

```diff
--- a/functions/index.js
+++ b/functions/index.js
@@ -4030,15 +4030,15 @@
                         }
                     }
                 } else {
-                    // Other general admin assets
-                    const [userSnap, staffAccSnap] = await Promise.all([
-                        db.collection('users').doc(uid).get(),
-                        db.collection('staff_accounts').doc(uid).get()
-                    ]);
-                    const adminDoc = staffAccSnap.exists ? staffAccSnap.data() : (userSnap.exists ? userSnap.data() : null);
-                    if (adminDoc && adminDoc.isDisabled !== true && adminDoc.isActive !== false) {
+                    // Other general admin assets: require explicit system/website/site management permission
+                    const allowedGeneralPrefixes = ['site_assets/', 'blog_assets/', 'portal_assets/'];
+                    const isAllowedPrefix = allowedGeneralPrefixes.some(p => normalizedPath.startsWith(p));
+                    if (isAllowedPrefix && (permissions.includes('MANAGE_SYSTEM') || permissions.includes('MANAGE_WEBSITE') || permissions.includes('MANAGE_SITES') || permissions.includes('SITES_MANAGE'))) {
                         isAuthorized = true;
                     }
                 }
```

#### `UDF-AUD-434` — **HIGH** | Regression / Security Bypass — `hashCandidatePasswordServer` and `checkGlobalUniqueness` check non-existent `token.provider_id === 'anonymous'` instead of `token.firebase.sign_in_provider === 'anonymous'`

- **Target File(s):** `functions/index.js` (`functions/index.js:79-81, 100-102`)
- **Root Cause & Exact Line Trace:** To remediate UDF-AUD-321 and UDF-AUD-322, lines 79 and 100 added `request.auth.token && request.auth.token.provider_id === 'anonymous'`. In Firebase Admin SDK `DecodedIdToken`, the sign-in provider is nested at `request.auth.token.firebase.sign_in_provider` (as correctly checked in `sendNotificationServer` at L8931), while `request.auth.token.provider_id` is `undefined` on real Firebase Auth ID tokens.
- **Real-World Impact:** Anonymous Firebase Auth sessions (`signInAnonymously()`) completely bypass the anonymous-user block on both endpoints, allowing unauthenticated visitors to abuse `hashCandidatePasswordServer` as a bcrypt CPU oracle and `checkGlobalUniqueness` to enumerate staff/employee emails and UIDs.
- **Copy-Paste `diff` Remediation:**

```diff
--- a/functions/index.js
+++ b/functions/index.js
@@ -76,7 +76,7 @@
 // Secure server-side password hashing using bcrypt (cost 12)
 exports.hashCandidatePasswordServer = secureOnCall(async (request) => {
-    if (!request.auth || (request.auth.token && request.auth.token.provider_id === 'anonymous')) {
+    if (!request.auth || request.auth.token?.firebase?.sign_in_provider === 'anonymous' || request.auth.token?.provider_id === 'anonymous') {
         throw new functions.https.HttpsError('unauthenticated', 'Must be authenticated with a verified non-anonymous account');
     }
@@ -97,7 +97,7 @@
 // Check global uniqueness across all identity collections
 exports.checkGlobalUniqueness = secureOnCall(async (request) => {
     // Only authenticated users can check uniqueness (prevents public scraping)
-    if (!request.auth || (request.auth.token && request.auth.token.provider_id === 'anonymous')) {
+    if (!request.auth || request.auth.token?.firebase?.sign_in_provider === 'anonymous' || request.auth.token?.provider_id === 'anonymous') {
         throw new functions.https.HttpsError('unauthenticated', 'Verification rejected: Non-anonymous authentication required.');
     }
```

#### `UDF-AUD-435` — **HIGH** | Security Vulnerability / Authorization Bypass — `completeBgvReviewServer` trusts caller-supplied `employeeId` over `bgvData.employeeId` and allows any `BGV_UPLOAD_CERTIFICATE` holder (including vendors) to approve/reject BGV

- **Target File(s):** `functions/index.js` (`functions/index.js:9041-9044, 9057, 9071-9075, 9115-9146`)
- **Root Cause & Exact Line Trace:** Line 9072 computes `const targetEmpId = employeeId || bgvData.employeeId;`, prioritizing untrusted client `request.data.employeeId` over the authoritative `bgvData.employeeId` stored on the `bgv_records/{recordId}` document, and then updates `empSnap` (`bgvStatus: 'Completed' | 'Failed'`) at L9116-9121 / L9141-9146. In addition, L9041-9044 and L9057 grant full `APPROVE`/`REJECT` authority to callers holding only `BGV_UPLOAD_CERTIFICATE` (such as external BGV vendors) without verifying `bgvData.vendorId === request.auth.uid`.
- **Real-World Impact:** An external BGV vendor or staff member with certificate-upload permission can self-approve or fail BGV clearance, and by passing a different `employeeId` in `request.data` can alter `bgvStatus` on an unrelated employee record without matching the BGV case.
- **Copy-Paste `diff` Remediation:**

```diff
--- a/functions/index.js
+++ b/functions/index.js
@@ -9039,9 +9039,8 @@
     const isSuperAdmin = token.isSuperAdmin === true || token.roleId === 'role-super-admin' || token.roleId === 'super-admin';
     const permissions = Array.isArray(token.permissions) ? token.permissions : [];
     let hasBgvPerm = isSuperAdmin ||
         permissions.includes('BGV_APPROVE_REJECT') ||
-        permissions.includes('BGV_MANAGE') ||
-        permissions.includes('BGV_UPLOAD_CERTIFICATE');
+        permissions.includes('BGV_MANAGE');
@@ -9054,7 +9053,7 @@
         if (!allowed && saData.roleId) {
             const roleSnap = await db.collection('roles').doc(saData.roleId).get();
             const rolePerms = (roleSnap.exists && Array.isArray(roleSnap.data().permissions)) ? roleSnap.data().permissions : [];
-            allowed = rolePerms.includes('BGV_APPROVE_REJECT') || rolePerms.includes('BGV_MANAGE') || rolePerms.includes('BGV_UPLOAD_CERTIFICATE');
+            allowed = rolePerms.includes('BGV_APPROVE_REJECT') || rolePerms.includes('BGV_MANAGE');
         }
@@ -9069,7 +9068,10 @@
     const bgvData = bgvSnap.data();
-    const targetEmpId = employeeId || bgvData.employeeId;
+    const targetEmpId = bgvData.employeeId;
+    if (employeeId && bgvData.employeeId && employeeId.trim() !== String(bgvData.employeeId).trim()) {
+        throw new functions.https.HttpsError('invalid-argument', 'employeeId does not match the BGV record.');
+    }
```

#### `UDF-AUD-436` — **HIGH** | Security Vulnerability / Authorization Bypass — `submitCorrectionServer` skips `email_verified === true` on owner email matching, lacks `isDisabled`/site checks for admin bypass, and completes requests non-transactionally

- **Target File(s):** `functions/index.js` (`functions/index.js:4772-4781, 4862-4891`)
- **Root Cause & Exact Line Trace:** While `verifyCorrectionIdentity` (L4606) was fixed to require `request.auth.token.email_verified === true`, `submitCorrectionServer` (L4774-4776) still authorizes any authenticated user whose unverified `request.auth.token.email` matches `targetData.linkedGoogleEmail || targetData.email || reqData.linkedGoogleEmail` (bypassing HMAC `sessionToken` verification completely). Additionally, L4777 grants `isAdmin` bypass from `token.permissions` without checking `isDisabled` or `sitesManaged`, and L4862-4891 marks the correction `completed` in a `db.batch()` rather than a transaction, allowing concurrent replays.
- **Real-World Impact:** An attacker with an unverified Firebase Auth account matching a candidate's email can submit arbitrary field corrections on pending correction requests without knowing the candidate's DOB/phone or HMAC session token.
- **Copy-Paste `diff` Remediation:**

```diff
--- a/functions/index.js
+++ b/functions/index.js
@@ -4771,10 +4771,11 @@
     if (!isAuthorized && request.auth) {
         const callerUid = request.auth.uid;
-        const callerEmail = (request.auth.token?.email || '').toLowerCase().trim();
+        const emailVerified = request.auth.token?.email_verified === true;
+        const callerEmail = emailVerified ? (request.auth.token?.email || '').toLowerCase().trim() : '';
         const targetEmail = (targetData.linkedGoogleEmail || targetData.email || reqData.linkedGoogleEmail || '').toLowerCase().trim();
         const isOwner = callerUid === targetData.uid || callerUid === targetData.linkedUid || (callerEmail && callerEmail === targetEmail);
-        const isAdmin = request.auth.token?.isSuperAdmin === true || (Array.isArray(request.auth.token?.permissions) && request.auth.token.permissions.includes('SUBMISSIONS_CREATE_CORRECTION_LINK'));
+        const isAdmin = request.auth.token?.isDisabled !== true && (request.auth.token?.isSuperAdmin === true || (Array.isArray(request.auth.token?.permissions) && request.auth.token.permissions.includes('SUBMISSIONS_CREATE_CORRECTION_LINK')));
         if (isOwner || isAdmin) {
             isAuthorized = true;
         }
     }
```

#### `UDF-AUD-437` — **HIGH** | Security Vulnerability / Authorization Bypass — `rejoinEmployeeServer` omits `isDisabled`/`isActive` checks in its Firestore fallback and only site-scopes the caller-supplied target `siteId` instead of `origData.currentSiteId`

- **Target File(s):** `functions/index.js` (`functions/index.js:6192-6235`)
- **Root Cause & Exact Line Trace:** The Firestore permission fallback added for UDF-AUD-241 at L6194-6212 never checks `saData.isDisabled === true || saData.isActive === false` (unlike `approveOnboardingServer` L5683 and `relieveEmployeeServer` L6028). Furthermore, L6225-6235 checks site authority only against `resolvedSiteId = siteId || origData.currentSiteId` — when a branch supervisor supplies their own `siteId` in `request.data`, the function never verifies whether they have authority over the original employee's site (`origData.currentSiteId`), and `isSuperAdmin` is `const` at L6184 so a SuperAdmin resolved via Firestore fallback at L6197 still fails the site check at L6226.
- **Real-World Impact:** Disabled staff accounts can still rejoin employees via the Firestore fallback, and a branch supervisor can pull/rejoin relieved employees belonging to other branches by passing their own `siteId` as the target site.
- **Copy-Paste `diff` Remediation:**

```diff
--- a/functions/index.js
+++ b/functions/index.js
@@ -6181,7 +6181,7 @@
     if (token.isDisabled === true) {
         throw new functions.https.HttpsError('permission-denied', 'Account is disabled.');
     }
-    const isSuperAdmin = token.isSuperAdmin === true || token.roleId === 'super-admin' || token.roleId === 'role-super-admin';
+    let isSuperAdmin = token.isSuperAdmin === true || token.roleId === 'super-admin' || token.roleId === 'role-super-admin';
     const permissions = Array.isArray(token.permissions) ? token.permissions : [];
@@ -6194,8 +6194,12 @@
         const saSnap = await db.collection('staff_accounts').doc(uid).get();
         if (saSnap.exists) {
             const saData = saSnap.data();
+            if (saData.isDisabled === true || saData.isActive === false) {
+                throw new functions.https.HttpsError('permission-denied', 'Account is disabled or inactive.');
+            }
             if (saData.roleId === 'role-super-admin' || saData.roleId === 'super-admin') {
+                isSuperAdmin = true;
                 hasRejoinPerm = true;
             } else if (saData.roleId) {
@@ -6224,12 +6228,16 @@
     const resolvedSiteId = siteId || origData.currentSiteId || 'Unassigned';
+    const origSiteId = origData.currentSiteId || origData.siteId || '';
     if (!isSuperAdmin && resolvedSiteId && resolvedSiteId !== 'Unassigned') {
         const hasGlobalScope = permissions.includes('EMPLOYEES_VIEW_SCOPE_ALL') ||
             permissions.includes('SUBMISSIONS_VIEW_SCOPE_ALL') ||
             permissions.includes('MANAGE_SITES') ||
             permissions.includes('MANAGE_ALL_SITES');
         const sitesManaged = Array.isArray(token.sitesManaged) ? token.sitesManaged : [];
         if (!hasGlobalScope && !sitesManaged.includes(resolvedSiteId)) {
             throw new functions.https.HttpsError('permission-denied', `You do not have management authority for site "${resolvedSiteId}".`);
         }
+        if (!hasGlobalScope && origSiteId && origSiteId !== 'Unassigned' && !sitesManaged.includes(origSiteId)) {
+            throw new functions.https.HttpsError('permission-denied', `You do not have management authority for the employee's original site "${origSiteId}".`);
+        }
     }
```

#### `UDF-AUD-438` — **HIGH** | Broken Feature / Runtime Crash & Data Duplication — `bulkImportEmployeesServer` exceeds Firestore's 500-op WriteBatch limit (`CHUNK_SIZE = 400` x 2 writes = 800 ops) and creates duplicate docs in `overwrite` mode

- **Target File(s):** `functions/index.js` (`functions/index.js:8349-8353, 8396, 8455-8486`)
- **Root Cause & Exact Line Trace:** Line 8349 sets `CHUNK_SIZE = 400`, while each row executes 2 writes (`batch.set(empRef, ...)` at L8467 and `batch.set(subRef, ...)` at L8468, incrementing `batchOps += 2` at L8476). A full chunk reaches 800 write operations, exceeding Firestore's hard limit of 500 operations per `WriteBatch` and throwing `INVALID_ARGUMENT` at `batch.commit()` (L8485). Additionally, in `overwriteMode === 'overwrite'`, L8396 and L8455 always mint a brand-new random `submissionId` instead of looking up the existing employee document ID, inserting duplicate employee documents with the same `employeeId`.
- **Real-World Impact:** Any bulk import with more than 250 valid rows deterministically crashes at commit time, and re-importing existing employees with `overwriteMode: 'overwrite'` duplicates their records instead of updating them.
- **Copy-Paste `diff` Remediation:**

```diff
--- a/functions/index.js
+++ b/functions/index.js
@@ -8346,7 +8346,7 @@
     // Process in batches of 400
-    const CHUNK_SIZE = 400;
+    const CHUNK_SIZE = 200; // 200 rows * 2 writes = 400 ops (safely below Firestore's 500-write batch limit)
     for (let c = 0; c < rows.length; c += CHUNK_SIZE) {
@@ -8452,16 +8452,21 @@
-                const empRef = db.collection('employees').doc(submissionId);
-                const subRef = db.collection('onboarding_submissions').doc(submissionId);
-
                 // Skip existing employee documents when overwriteMode is 'skip' (UDF13-05)
+                let targetDocId = submissionId;
+                const existingEmpSnap = await db.collection('employees').where('employeeId', '==', empId).limit(1).get();
                 if (overwriteMode === 'skip') {
-                    const existingEmpSnap = await db.collection('employees').where('employeeId', '==', empId).limit(1).get();
                     if (!existingEmpSnap.empty) {
                         warnings.push({ row: rowNum, warning: `Skipped existing employee ID "${empId}".` });
                         continue;
                     }
+                } else if (overwriteMode === 'overwrite' && !existingEmpSnap.empty) {
+                    targetDocId = existingEmpSnap.docs[0].id;
+                    employeeRecord.portalId = existingEmpSnap.docs[0].data().portalId || targetDocId;
                 }
+                const empRef = db.collection('employees').doc(targetDocId);
+                const subRef = db.collection('onboarding_submissions').doc(targetDocId);
```

#### `UDF-AUD-439` — **HIGH** | Regression / Broken Feature — uploadProfilePhoto writes staff avatars to staff_accounts/ path rejected by getUploadUrl Cloud Function

- **Target File(s):** `src/services/storageService.ts`, `functions/index.js` (`src/services/storageService.ts:491-505, functions/index.js:3800-3802`)
- **Root Cause & Exact Line Trace:** To address UDF-AUD-164, uploadProfilePhoto (storageService.ts:498-501) now constructs storagePath = `staff_accounts/${userId}/profile/avatar.jpg` when userType === 'staff_accounts' and calls uploadViaSignedUrl('profile', storagePath, compressed). However, functions/index.js:3800-3802 enforces `if (context === 'profile' && firstSegment !== 'profile' && firstSegment !== 'employees') throw invalid-argument`, rejecting every staff avatar upload.
- **Real-World Impact:** 100% of staff and admin profile photo uploads fail immediately with `invalid-argument: Profile uploads must reside under profile/ or employees/.`
- **Copy-Paste `diff` Remediation:**

```diff
--- a/functions/index.js
+++ b/functions/index.js
@@ -3797,5 +3797,5 @@
     if (context === 'employee' && firstSegment !== 'employees') {
         throw new functions.https.HttpsError('invalid-argument', 'Employee uploads must reside under employees/.');
     }
-    if (context === 'profile' && firstSegment !== 'profile' && firstSegment !== 'employees') {
-        throw new functions.https.HttpsError('invalid-argument', 'Profile uploads must reside under profile/ or employees/.');
+    if (context === 'profile' && firstSegment !== 'profile' && firstSegment !== 'employees' && firstSegment !== 'staff_accounts') {
+        throw new functions.https.HttpsError('invalid-argument', 'Profile uploads must reside under profile/, employees/, or staff_accounts/.');
     }
```

#### `UDF-AUD-440` — **HIGH** | Regression / Client-to-Callable Payload Mismatch — driveService.renameEmployeeFolder and approveEmployeeFolder pass Drive folder display names as Firestore recordId to syncEmployeeToGoogleDriveServer

- **Target File(s):** `src/services/driveService.ts`, `src/services/employeeService.ts`, `functions/index.js` (`src/services/driveService.ts:31-50, src/services/employeeService.ts:2403-2407, functions/index.js:7244-7250`)
- **Root Cause & Exact Line Trace:** In udf21, renameEmployeeFolder(oldName, newName) (driveService.ts:31-39) and approveEmployeeFolder(oldFolderName, newFolderName) (L41-50) were wired to call syncEmployeeToGoogleDriveServer with `{ recordId: newName, oldName }` and `syncEmployeeToGoogleDrive(newFolderName, 'employee')`. Callers such as revertSubmissionWorkflow (employeeService.ts:2404-2407) pass formatted Drive folder names like `"UDF-202603-XXXX - John Doe"`, whereas syncEmployeeToGoogleDriveServer (functions/index.js:7244-7250) expects a Firestore document ID and completely ignores `oldName`.
- **Real-World Impact:** Every Drive folder rename/approval sync fails server-side with document-not-found (silently swallowed in catch), leaving Google Drive folders out of sync after onboarding approvals or reverts.
- **Copy-Paste `diff` Remediation:**

```diff
--- a/src/services/driveService.ts
+++ b/src/services/driveService.ts
@@ -31,20 +31,22 @@
 export const renameEmployeeFolder = async (oldName: string, newName: string): Promise<void> => {
     log.log(`[DRIVE_SERVICE] Renaming folder: ${oldName} -> ${newName} (server-managed).`);
     try {
+        const recordId = newName.split(' - ')[0]?.trim() || newName;
         const fn = httpsCallable(functions, 'syncEmployeeToGoogleDriveServer');
-        await fn({ recordId: newName, oldName });
+        await fn({ recordId, targetType: 'employee' });
     } catch (e) {
         log.warn('[DRIVE_SERVICE] renameEmployeeFolder server sync warning:', e);
     }
 };
 
 export const approveEmployeeFolder = async (oldFolderName: string, newFolderName: string): Promise<void> => {
     log.log(`[DRIVE_SERVICE] Approve folder: ${oldFolderName} -> ${newFolderName}`);
     if (newFolderName) {
         try {
-            await syncEmployeeToGoogleDrive(newFolderName, 'employee');
+            const recordId = newFolderName.split(' - ')[0]?.trim() || newFolderName;
+            await syncEmployeeToGoogleDrive(recordId, 'employee');
         } catch (e) {
             log.warn('[DRIVE_SERVICE] approveEmployeeFolder sync warning:', e);
         }
     }
 };
```

#### `UDF-AUD-441` — **HIGH** | Regression / Broken Feature & Missing Index — updatePublicStats queries (isActive == true, isRelieved == false) with no composite index in firestore.indexes.json and drops legacy active employees

- **Target File(s):** `src/services/statsService.ts`, `firestore.indexes.json` (`src/services/statsService.ts:33-36`)
- **Root Cause & Exact Line Trace:** When updating updatePublicStats for UDF-AUD-162, statsService.ts:34 changed the query to `query(empColl, where('isActive', '==', true), where('isRelieved', '==', false))`. No composite index for `employees(isActive ASC, isRelieved ASC)` exists in firestore.indexes.json, causing getCountFromServer to fail with FAILED_PRECONDITION. Additionally, Firestore equality queries on `isRelieved == false` exclude legacy active employee documents where `isRelieved` is omitted/undefined.
- **Real-World Impact:** updatePublicStats() fails on every invocation due to the missing composite index; even after creating the index, legacy active employees missing an explicit `isRelieved: false` field are undercounted on the public website.
- **Copy-Paste `diff` Remediation:**

```diff
--- a/firestore.indexes.json
+++ b/firestore.indexes.json
@@ -1,4 +1,11 @@
 {
   "indexes": [
+    {
+      "collectionGroup": "employees",
+      "queryScope": "COLLECTION",
+      "fields": [
+        { "fieldPath": "isActive", "order": "ASCENDING" },
+        { "fieldPath": "isRelieved", "order": "ASCENDING" }
+      ]
+    },
```

#### `UDF-AUD-442` — **HIGH** | Broken Feature / Infinite Redirect Loop Regression — AuthGuard Redirects Unauthenticated `/employee/*` Visitors to `/employee/login` Which Loops Infinitely on `employee.udfonline.in` & Drops `?redirect=` Deep-Link Param

- **Target File(s):** `src/components/guards/AuthGuard.tsx`, `src/routes/AppRoutes.tsx` (`AuthGuard.tsx:29-35, AppRoutes.tsx:217-258`)
- **Root Cause & Exact Line Trace:** The `UDF-AUD-21` patch in `AuthGuard.tsx:31-34` redirects unauthenticated/guest users on `/employee/*` paths to `/employee/login`. However, when `VITE_APP_TARGET === 'employee'` (`AppRoutes.tsx:217-258`), there is NO `/employee/login` route—only `/`, `/login`, `/employee/dashboard`, and `<Route path="/employee/*" element={<AuthGuard ...>}>` (L248). Navigating to `/employee/login` matches `/employee/*`, mounts `AuthGuard` again, and redirects to `/employee/login` in an infinite loop. The patch also dropped `?redirect=${encodeURIComponent(currentPath)}`, breaking post-login deep-link restoration at `AppRoutes.tsx:227`.
- **Real-World Impact:** Any unauthenticated or guest user visiting an `/employee/...` link on `employee.udfonline.in` hits an infinite browser redirect loop instead of the login page, and deep links lose their return path after login.
- **Copy-Paste `diff` Remediation:**

```diff
--- a/src/routes/AppRoutes.tsx
+++ b/src/routes/AppRoutes.tsx
@@ -245,6 +245,7 @@
                         />
                     } />
+                    <Route path="/employee/login" element={<Navigate to="/login" replace />} />
                     <Route path="/employee/dashboard" element={<Navigate to="/dashboard" replace />} />
                     <Route path="/employee/*" element={
--- a/src/components/guards/AuthGuard.tsx
+++ b/src/components/guards/AuthGuard.tsx
@@ -29,9 +29,11 @@
     if (isPublicOrGuest) {
         const isPathAdmin = location.pathname.startsWith('/admin');
         const isPathEmployee = location.pathname.startsWith('/employee');
-        const redirectPath = isPathAdmin ? '/admin/login' : (isPathEmployee ? '/employee/login' : '/login');
-        console.warn(`AuthGuard: Guest or unauthenticated user on protected path ${location.pathname}. Redirecting to ${redirectPath}`);
-        return <Navigate to={redirectPath} state={{ from: location }} replace />;
+        const baseLogin = isPathAdmin ? '/admin/login' : '/login';
+        const currentPath = location.pathname + location.search;
+        const loginUrl = currentPath && currentPath !== '/' && currentPath !== '/login' && currentPath !== '/employee/login'
+            ? `${baseLogin}?redirect=${encodeURIComponent(currentPath)}`
+            : baseLogin;
+        return <Navigate to={loginUrl} state={{ from: location }} replace />;
     }
```

#### `UDF-AUD-443` — **HIGH** | Security / Stale Session Subscription Regression — App.tsx `hasSubmissionsSubscribedRef` Never Resets When `authUser.id` or `authUser.type` Changes Without an Intermediate `null` Render

- **Target File(s):** `App.tsx` (`App.tsx:1141-1186`)
- **Root Cause & Exact Line Trace:** The `UDF-AUD-61` patch introduced `hasSubmissionsSubscribedRef` and `submissionsUnsubRef` to avoid tearing down listeners on tab switch, but only resets `hasSubmissionsSubscribedRef.current = false` inside `if (!authUser)` (L1145-1151). If `authUser` transitions directly from one user/role to another (e.g., account switch or re-auth replacing `auth.currentUser`) without passing through `authUser === null`, the previous user's `subscribeToSubmissions` or `subscribeToSubmissionsByUid(oldUser.id)` listener remains active and the new user never subscribes.
- **Real-World Impact:** Account switches without full logout can leak the previous user's `submittedForms` into the new session or leave an Admin/Employee with stale submission data.
- **Copy-Paste `diff` Remediation:**

```diff
--- a/App.tsx
+++ b/App.tsx
@@ -1140,12 +1140,18 @@
   // EMPLOYEES get only their own submissions
   const hasSubmissionsSubscribedRef = useRef(false);
   const submissionsUnsubRef = useRef<() => void>(() => {});
+  const subscribedKeyRef = useRef<string | null>(null);
 
   useEffect(() => {
-    if (!authUser) {
+    const currentKey = authUser ? `${authUser.type}:${authUser.id}` : null;
+    if (!authUser || (subscribedKeyRef.current && subscribedKeyRef.current !== currentKey)) {
       hasSubmissionsSubscribedRef.current = false;
+      subscribedKeyRef.current = null;
       submissionsUnsubRef.current();
       submissionsUnsubRef.current = () => {};
       setSubmittedForms([]);
-      return;
+      if (!authUser) return;
     }
+    subscribedKeyRef.current = currentKey;
```

#### `UDF-AUD-445` — **HIGH** | Broken Feature / Query Mismatch Regression — subscribeToBgvRecords Orders by Non-Existent `initiatedDate` Field, Silently Returning 0 BGV Records for SuperAdmins & Global Viewers

- **Target File(s):** `src/services/bgvService.ts`, `components/AdminBgvManagementPage.tsx` (`bgvService.ts:27-31, 265-284; AdminBgvManagementPage.tsx:135-143`)
- **Root Cause & Exact Line Trace:** bgvService.ts:28 builds the default BGV listener query as `query(collection(db, BGV_COLLECTION), orderBy("initiatedDate", "desc"), limit(500))`. However, `syncBgvCandidates` (bgvService.ts:265-284) writes `createdAt` and `updatedAt` onto `BgvRecord` documents and NEVER writes an `initiatedDate` field. In Firestore, any `orderBy(field)` query silently excludes all documents missing that field. Additionally, when `allowedSiteIds` is `[]` (unassigned staff), L29 (`allowedSiteIds.length > 0`) falls through to the unscoped query instead of returning `[]` immediately.
- **Real-World Impact:** SuperAdmins and staff with `BGV_VIEW_SCOPE_ALL` see an empty BGV Clearance table (`0` records) for all synced candidates, while site-scoped staff with `allowedSiteIds.length > 0` bypass `orderBy` and `limit(500)` completely.
- **Copy-Paste `diff` Remediation:**

```diff
--- a/src/services/bgvService.ts
+++ b/src/services/bgvService.ts
@@ -25,9 +25,13 @@
  */
 export const subscribeToBgvRecords = (callback: (records: BgvRecord[]) => void, allowedSiteIds?: string[]) => {
-  let q = query(collection(db, BGV_COLLECTION), orderBy('initiatedDate', 'desc'), limit(500));
-  if (allowedSiteIds && allowedSiteIds.length > 0) {
-    q = query(collection(db, BGV_COLLECTION), where('siteId', 'in', allowedSiteIds.slice(0, 30)));
+  if (allowedSiteIds !== undefined && allowedSiteIds.length === 0) {
+    callback([]);
+    return () => {};
+  }
+  let q = query(collection(db, BGV_COLLECTION), orderBy('createdAt', 'desc'), limit(500));
+  if (allowedSiteIds && allowedSiteIds.length > 0) {
+    q = query(collection(db, BGV_COLLECTION), where('siteId', 'in', allowedSiteIds.slice(0, 30)), limit(500));
   }
```

#### `UDF-AUD-448` — **MEDIUM** | Broken Feature / Rules-Client Mismatch Regression — `AdminWebsiteManager.tsx` unscoped `attendance` query fails with `PERMISSION_DENIED` for site-scoped admins after `canStaffViewSite` was added to `firestore.rules:617`

Because Firestore rules do not act as query filters, `AdminWebsiteManager.tsx:140` (`query(collection(db, 'attendance'), orderBy('date', 'desc'), limit(200))`) fails for any site-scoped admin who lacks a `*_VIEW_SCOPE_ALL` permission.
- **Real-World Impact:** Branch supervisors and site-scoped admins opening the Attendance tab in `AdminWebsiteManager` receive `FirebaseError: Missing or insufficient permissions` and cannot view their own branch's attendance logs.
- **Copy-Paste `diff` Remediation:**

```diff
--- a/components/AdminWebsiteManager.tsx
+++ b/components/AdminWebsiteManager.tsx
@@ -137,7 +137,11 @@
     const loadAttendanceRecords = async () => {
         try {
-            const snap = await getDocs(query(collection(db, 'attendance'), orderBy('date', 'desc'), limit(200)));
+            const siteIds = (!isSuperAdmin && managedSiteIds && managedSiteIds.length > 0) ? managedSiteIds.slice(0, 30) : null;
+            const q = siteIds
+                ? query(collection(db, 'attendance'), where('siteId', 'in', siteIds), orderBy('date', 'desc'), limit(200))
+                : query(collection(db, 'attendance'), orderBy('date', 'desc'), limit(200));
```
