```diff
             const list = snap.docs.map(doc => ({ id: doc.id, ...doc.data() }));
             setAttendanceRecords(list);
```

#### `UDF-AUD-449` — **MEDIUM** | Build & PWA Config Bug — `vite.config.ts` `generate-version-json` defaults to `dist-public` while `build.outDir` defaults to `dist`, and `navigateFallbackDenylist` omits `/api/*`

- **Target File(s):** `vite.config.ts` (`vite.config.ts:55-58, 123-131, 141-148`)
- **Root Cause & Exact Line Trace:** When `VITE_APP_TARGET` is unset (`npm run build`), `generate-version-json` (L56-57) defaults `target` to `'public'` and attempts to write `dist-public/version.json`, whereas `build.outDir` (L142-148) outputs to `'dist'`. Additionally, `workbox.navigateFallbackDenylist` (L123-131) omits `/^\/api\/.*$/`, causing the PWA Service Worker to intercept direct browser navigations to `/api/*` Cloud Function endpoints and serve `index.html`.
- **Real-World Impact:** Default `npm run build` outputs lack `dist/version.json` (causing 404s on version-polling), and direct navigations to `/api/*` endpoints on PWA-enabled portals are hijacked by the SPA fallback.
- **Copy-Paste `diff` Remediation:**

```diff
--- a/vite.config.ts
+++ b/vite.config.ts
@@ -53,8 +53,8 @@
       {
         name: 'generate-version-json',
         closeBundle() {
-          const target = env.VITE_APP_TARGET || process.env.VITE_APP_TARGET || 'public';
-          const outDir = target === 'admin' ? 'dist-admin' : target === 'employee' ? 'dist-employee' : 'dist-public';
+          const target = env.VITE_APP_TARGET || process.env.VITE_APP_TARGET;
+          const outDir = target === 'admin' ? 'dist-admin' : target === 'employee' ? 'dist-employee' : target === 'public' ? 'dist-public' : 'dist';
           const versionPath = path.resolve(__dirname, outDir, 'version.json');
@@ -127,7 +127,8 @@
             /^\/llms\.txt$/,
             /^\/google8aaac7081881f131\.html$/,
             /^\/UDF_Brochure\.pdf$/,
-            /^\/__\/.*$/
+            /^\/__\/.*$/,
+            /^\/api\/.*$/
           ],
```

#### `UDF-AUD-450` — **MEDIUM** | Regression / Data Loss — completeTrainingQuizServer deletes duplicate `user_engagement/{uid}` without migrating existing points to `user_engagement/{targetDocId}`

- **Target File(s):** `functions/index.js` (`functions/index.js:6645-6660`)
- **Root Cause & Exact Line Trace:** To fix UDF-AUD-177, `recordDailyStreakServer` (L8598-8603, L8754-8756) and `redeemRewardServer` (L8858-8861, L8879-8881) read `user_engagement/{uid}` inside a transaction and migrate its points before deleting it. However, `completeTrainingQuizServer` (L6657-6659) blindly executes `batch.delete(db.collection('user_engagement').doc(uid))` without first reading `user_engagement/{uid}` or merging its points into `user_engagement/{targetDocId}`.
- **Real-World Impact:** If an employee with legacy points stored under `user_engagement/{uid}` completes a safety quiz before triggering a daily streak update, their previously earned points in `user_engagement/{uid}` are permanently deleted.
- **Copy-Paste `diff` Remediation:**

```diff
--- a/functions/index.js
+++ b/functions/index.js
@@ -6648,14 +6648,22 @@
     const batch = db.batch();
 
-    batch.set(engagementRef, {
-        points: admin.firestore.FieldValue.increment(quizPoints),
+    let migratedPoints = 0;
+    if (targetDocId !== uid) {
+        const uidEngSnap = await db.collection('user_engagement').doc(uid).get();
+        if (uidEngSnap.exists) {
+            if (!engagementDocCheck.exists) {
+                migratedPoints = Number(uidEngSnap.data().points || 0);
+            }
+            batch.delete(uidEngSnap.ref);
+        }
+    }
+
+    batch.set(engagementRef, {
+        points: admin.firestore.FieldValue.increment(quizPoints + migratedPoints),
         lastQuizDate: today,
         lastUpdated: admin.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp()
     }, { merge: true });
-
-    // Clean up duplicate uid document if targetDocId !== uid to prevent duplicate leaderboard entries (UDF-AUD-177)
-    if (targetDocId !== uid) {
-        batch.delete(db.collection('user_engagement').doc(uid));
-    }
```

#### `UDF-AUD-451` — **MEDIUM** | Functional Bug / Error Masking — deleteStaffUser, moveStorageFolder, and getSystemMetrics swallow HttpsError('permission-denied') inside catch blocks and rethrow HttpsError('internal')

- **Target File(s):** `functions/index.js` (`functions/index.js:371-395, 2415-2519, 3308-3438`)
- **Root Cause & Exact Line Trace:** In `deleteStaffUser` (L376-378 -> catch L392-395), `moveStorageFolder` (L2430, L2443, L2482 -> catch L2516-2519), and `getSystemMetrics` (L3324 -> catch L3435-3438), `HttpsError('permission-denied', ...)` / `HttpsError('invalid-argument', ...)` are thrown inside `try` blocks whose `catch` handlers omit `if (error instanceof functions.https.HttpsError) throw error;` and unconditionally wrap every error in `HttpsError('internal', ...)`.
- **Real-World Impact:** Clients receive generic `internal` (HTTP 500) errors instead of `permission-denied` (HTTP 403), breaking client-side authorization error handling and polluting Cloud Functions error monitoring with false 5xx alerts.
- **Copy-Paste `diff` Remediation:**

```diff
--- a/functions/index.js
+++ b/functions/index.js
@@ -390,6 +390,7 @@
         await admin.firestore().collection('users').doc(uid).delete().catch(() => {});
         return { success: true, message: 'User deleted successfully' };
     } catch (error) {
+        if (error instanceof functions.https.HttpsError) throw error;
         console.error('Error deleting user:', error);
         throw new functions.https.HttpsError('internal', error.message || 'Internal server error');
     }
@@ -2514,6 +2515,7 @@
         await Promise.all(movePromises);
         return { success: true };
     } catch (error) {
+        if (error instanceof functions.https.HttpsError) throw error;
         console.error('Error moving storage folder:', error);
         throw new functions.https.HttpsError('internal', 'Failed to move storage folder.');
     }
@@ -3433,6 +3435,7 @@
         };
 
     } catch (error) {
+        if (error instanceof functions.https.HttpsError) throw error;
         console.error('getSystemMetrics failed:', error);
         throw new functions.https.HttpsError('internal', 'Failed to retrieve system metrics.');
     }
```

#### `UDF-AUD-452` — **MEDIUM** | Broken Feature / Functional Bug — `syncSubmissionToSheetsServer` unconditionally marks `googleFormSyncStatus: 'synced'` even when Sheets/Form sync fails, permanently blocking retries

- **Target File(s):** `functions/index.js` (`functions/index.js:7018-7021, 7303-7308`)
- **Root Cause & Exact Line Trace:** Unlike `syncRecordToDriveAndSheetsCore` (L6979-6984), `syncSubmissionToSheetsServer` (L7303-7308) ignores the return value of `appendSubmissionToSheetsServer` (`'form_sync_failed'`, `'form_sync_error'`, `'form_sync_not_configured'`) and unconditionally writes `googleFormSyncStatus: 'synced'`. On subsequent retries, `appendSubmissionToSheetsServer`'s idempotency guard at L7018 (`if (recordData.googleFormSyncStatus === 'synced') return 'already_synced'`) permanently skips the submission.
- **Real-World Impact:** Transient Google Sheets or Apps Script failures permanently mark the submission as `'synced'`, silently dropping onboarding records from the HR master spreadsheet with no way to retry via the sync button.
- **Copy-Paste `diff` Remediation:**

```diff
--- a/functions/index.js
+++ b/functions/index.js
@@ -7301,8 +7301,9 @@
         const status = await appendSubmissionToSheetsServer(data, formData, folderUrl);
+        const isSheetsSuccess = status === 'sheets_appended' || status === 'form_synced' || status === 'already_synced';
         await subSnap.ref.update({
-            googleFormSyncStatus: 'synced',
+            googleFormSyncStatus: isSheetsSuccess ? 'synced' : 'failed',
             googleFormSyncedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
             googleSheetsSyncStatus: status
         });
```

#### `UDF-AUD-453` — **MEDIUM** | Security Vulnerability / Race Condition — `completeTrainingQuizServer` checks daily quiz completion outside a transaction and awards points via `db.batch()`, allowing concurrent point farming

- **Target File(s):** `functions/index.js` (`functions/index.js:6576-6610, 6644-6672`)
- **Root Cause & Exact Line Trace:** `completeTrainingQuizServer` checks `gamification_logs` (L6577-6604) and `user_engagement/{targetDocId}.lastQuizDate` (L6607-6610) with standalone `.get()` reads, and then awards points using a non-transactional `db.batch()` with `FieldValue.increment(quizPoints)` (L6648-6672). In contrast, `recordDailyStreakServer` (L8591) and `redeemRewardServer` (L8851) wrap their checks and writes inside `db.runTransaction`.
- **Real-World Impact:** An employee submitting N parallel calls to `completeTrainingQuizServer` with the correct answer bypasses the once-per-day check and earns `N * quizPoints` in a single day, which can then be redeemed for physical rewards via `redeemRewardServer`.
- **Copy-Paste `diff` Remediation:**

```diff
--- a/functions/index.js
+++ b/functions/index.js
@@ -6644,27 +6644,31 @@
     // 4. Atomically update user_engagement points and write to gamification_logs
     const engagementRef = db.collection('user_engagement').doc(targetDocId);
-    const logRef = db.collection('gamification_logs').doc();
+    const logRef = db.collection('gamification_logs').doc(`quiz_${targetDocId}_${today}`);
 
-    const batch = db.batch();
-    batch.set(engagementRef, {
-        points: admin.firestore.FieldValue.increment(quizPoints),
-        lastQuizDate: today,
-        lastUpdated: admin.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp()
-    }, { merge: true });
-    if (targetDocId !== uid) {
-        batch.delete(db.collection('user_engagement').doc(uid));
-    }
-    batch.set(logRef, {
-        userId: targetDocId,
-        userUid: uid,
-        type: 'QUIZ',
-        questionId: questionId || 'daily_safety_quiz',
-        selectedAnswer,
-        pointsAwarded: quizPoints,
-        date: today,
-        timestamp: admin.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp()
-    });
-    await batch.commit();
+    await db.runTransaction(async (tx) => {
+        const [engSnap, existingQuizLog] = await Promise.all([tx.get(engagementRef), tx.get(logRef)]);
+        if (existingQuizLog.exists || (engSnap.exists && engSnap.data().lastQuizDate === today)) {
+            throw new functions.https.HttpsError('already-exists', 'You have already completed the daily quiz today.');
+        }
+        tx.set(engagementRef, {
+            points: admin.firestore.FieldValue.increment(quizPoints),
+            lastQuizDate: today,
+            lastUpdated: admin.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp()
+        }, { merge: true });
+        if (targetDocId !== uid) {
+            tx.delete(db.collection('user_engagement').doc(uid));
+        }
+        tx.set(logRef, {
+            userId: targetDocId,
+            userUid: uid,
+            type: 'QUIZ',
+            questionId: questionId || 'daily_safety_quiz',
+            selectedAnswer,
+            pointsAwarded: quizPoints,
+            date: today,
+            timestamp: admin.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp()
+        });
+    });
```

#### `UDF-AUD-454` — **MEDIUM** | Broken Feature / Data Integrity — `promoteEmployeeServer` overwrites `assignedSiteIds` with `[newSiteId]` on transfer, wiping secondary site assignments and desyncing from `activeAssignments`

- **Target File(s):** `functions/index.js` (`functions/index.js:8230-8247`)
- **Root Cause & Exact Line Trace:** When `newSiteId` is provided during a promotion, L8232 sets `updates.assignedSiteIds = [newSiteId]`, discarding all secondary site IDs. Meanwhile, L8236-8247 only updates the `type === 'PRIMARY'` entry inside `activeAssignments` and preserves all non-PRIMARY (`SECONDARY`) site entries.
- **Real-World Impact:** After promoting and transferring an employee who holds secondary site deployments, `assignedSiteIds` no longer contains their secondary sites, breaking site-scoped queries (`array-contains`) and hiding the employee from secondary branch supervisors even though their secondary `activeAssignments` remain active.

```diff
--- a/functions/index.js
+++ b/functions/index.js
@@ -8229,11 +8229,6 @@
-    if (newSiteId) {
-        updates.currentSiteId = newSiteId;
-        updates.assignedSiteIds = [newSiteId];
-        if (newSiteName) updates.siteName = newSiteName;
-    }
-
     if (Array.isArray(empData.activeAssignments) && empData.activeAssignments.length > 0) {
         updates.activeAssignments = empData.activeAssignments.map(a => {
             if (a.type === 'PRIMARY') {
@@ -8245,6 +8240,14 @@
             return a;
         });
     }
+
+    if (newSiteId) {
+        updates.currentSiteId = newSiteId;
+        const secondarySites = (updates.activeAssignments || empData.activeAssignments || [])
+            .filter(a => a && a.type !== 'PRIMARY' && a.siteId)
+            .map(a => a.siteId);
+        updates.assignedSiteIds = Array.from(new Set([newSiteId, ...secondarySites]));
+        if (newSiteName) updates.siteName = newSiteName;
+    }
```

#### `UDF-AUD-455` — **MEDIUM** | Regression / Dead Code & Data Integrity — syncBgvCandidates isRelieved update branch is unreachable dead code because activeEmployees filters out relieved employees earlier

- **Target File(s):** `src/services/bgvService.ts` (`src/services/bgvService.ts:161-162, 308-310`)
- **Root Cause & Exact Line Trace:** To address UDF-AUD-281, lines 308-310 added `if ((emp as any).isRelieved || (emp as any).status === 'Relieved') { updates.isRelieved = true; }` inside the `for (const emp of activeEmployees)` loop (L179). However, lines 161-162 already exclude every employee with `e.isActive === false || (e as any).isRelieved === true` from `activeEmployees`, making L308-310 unreachable for relieved employees.
- **Real-World Impact:** Relieved employees' existing bgv_records are never flagged `isRelieved: true` during BGV sync, keeping separated personnel visible in active BGV queues.
- **Copy-Paste `diff` Remediation:**

```diff
--- a/src/services/bgvService.ts
+++ b/src/services/bgvService.ts
@@ -161,8 +161,9 @@
   const activeEmployees = employeesList.filter(e => {
-    if (e.isActive === false || (e as any).isRelieved === true) return false;
+    const isRelieved = e.isActive === false || (e as any).isRelieved === true;
+    if (isRelieved && !existingMap.has(e.id) && !existingMap.has((e as any).employeeId)) return false;
```

#### `UDF-AUD-456` — **MEDIUM** | Regression / Resource Leak — getGoogleCredentialSafely leaks secondary Firebase App 'GoogleLinkerApp' when popup is closed or an error occurs

- **Target File(s):** `src/utils/googleAuthHelper.ts` (`src/utils/googleAuthHelper.ts:46-94`)
- **Root Cause & Exact Line Trace:** When adding deleteApp(secApp) cleanup for UDF-AUD-417, lines 63-67 (native) and 83-87 (web) placed `secAuth.signOut()` and `deleteApp(secApp)` strictly on the happy path before `return res` instead of inside a `finally` block. Any thrown error (such as user closing the popup or missing email at L60/L80) bypasses cleanup.
- **Real-World Impact:** Repeated failed or cancelled Google linking attempts leak the secondary Firebase App instance and leave its Auth state initialized in memory.
- **Copy-Paste `diff` Remediation:**

```diff
--- a/src/utils/googleAuthHelper.ts
+++ b/src/utils/googleAuthHelper.ts
@@ -74,19 +74,20 @@
         try {
             const secAuth = getSecondaryAuth();
             await setPersistence(secAuth, inMemoryPersistence);
             const provider = new GoogleAuthProvider();
             provider.setCustomParameters({ prompt: 'select_account' });
             const userCred = await signInWithPopup(secAuth, provider);
             if (!userCred.user.email) throw new Error('Google account did not return an email address.');
             const idToken = await userCred.user.getIdToken();
             const res = { uid: userCred.user.uid, email: userCred.user.email, idToken };
-            await secAuth.signOut();
-            try {
-                const secApp = getApp('GoogleLinkerApp');
-                await deleteApp(secApp);
-            } catch {}
             return res;
         } catch (error: any) {
             log.error('Web Safe Google error:', error);
             throw error;
+        } finally {
+            try {
+                const secApp = getApp('GoogleLinkerApp');
+                await getAuth(secApp).signOut().catch(() => {});
+                await deleteApp(secApp);
+            } catch {}
         }
```

#### `UDF-AUD-457` — **MEDIUM** | Regression / Broken Feature — markAllRead skips employee notifications targeted by portalId after filtering strictly by n.targetUserId === userId

- **Target File(s):** `src/services/notificationService.ts` (`src/services/notificationService.ts:47, 109-111`)
- **Root Cause & Exact Line Trace:** While fixing UDF-AUD-344 to exclude ROLE:* broadcast notifications, markAllRead (L110) narrowed the filter to `n.targetUserId === userId`. However, subscribeToNotifications (L47) fetches personal notifications addressed to both `userId` (Firebase UID) and `portalId` (submission/employee ID).
- **Real-World Impact:** Employees whose notifications are dispatched to their `portalId` cannot clear them via 'Mark All Read'; those notifications remain permanently unread until clicked individually.
- **Copy-Paste `diff` Remediation:**

```diff
--- a/src/services/notificationService.ts
+++ b/src/services/notificationService.ts
@@ -106,8 +106,9 @@
-export const markAllRead = async (userId: string, currentNotifs: Notification[]) => {
-    const unread = currentNotifs.filter(n => !n.read && n.targetUserId === userId).slice(0, 450);
+export const markAllRead = async (userId: string, currentNotifs: Notification[], portalId?: string) => {
+    const ownedTargets = new Set([userId, portalId].filter(Boolean));
+    const unread = currentNotifs.filter(n => !n.read && ownedTargets.has(n.targetUserId)).slice(0, 450);
```

#### `UDF-AUD-458` — **MEDIUM** | React Hook / Memory Leak Regression — AuthContext Real-Time Claim Listener Lacks Unmount Cancellation Guard Around Async Dynamic Imports

- **Target File(s):** `src/context/AuthContext.tsx` (`src/context/AuthContext.tsx:110-192`)
- **Root Cause & Exact Line Trace:** In `AuthContext.tsx:115-191`, `listenForClaimUpdates()` awaits dynamic imports (`import('../firebase/config')` and `import('firebase/firestore')`) before assigning `unsubStaff` and `unsubUsers`, with no `cancelled` boolean check. If `user?.id` changes or `AuthProvider` unmounts before the imports resolve, the cleanup callback runs while `unsubStaff` is `null`, leaking live Firestore `onSnapshot` listeners.
- **Real-World Impact:** Leaked `staff_accounts`/`users` snapshot listeners remain active after sign-out or user switch and can trigger spurious `auth.signOut()` or `setUser()` calls on a new session.
- **Copy-Paste `diff` Remediation:**

```diff
--- a/src/context/AuthContext.tsx
+++ b/src/context/AuthContext.tsx
@@ -112,11 +112,13 @@
 
         let unsubStaff: (() => void) | null = null;
         let unsubUsers: (() => void) | null = null;
+        let cancelled = false;
         const listenForClaimUpdates = async () => {
             try {
                 const { db } = await import('../firebase/config');
                 const { doc, onSnapshot } = await import('firebase/firestore');
+                if (cancelled) return;
@@ -186,6 +188,7 @@
 
         listenForClaimUpdates();
         return () => {
+            cancelled = true;
             if (unsubStaff) unsubStaff();
             if (unsubUsers) unsubUsers();
         };
```
